<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" dir="ltr">Two important things:</p>
<ol dir="ltr" class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-decimal flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3 print:block print:space-y-1">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><strong>Open a fresh terminal after running this</strong> — the current one still has the old value cached in its own session, same as every environment variable change tonight.</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><strong>BuildConsole itself needs a restart too</strong>, since it's a currently-running process that already inherited the old environment when it launched — a new environment variable value won't reach it until it's relaunched.</li>
</ol>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" dir="ltr">Once both are restarted, the secondary account should authenticate against its own <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">.claude-secondary</code> session properly, since there's no longer a global token to override it.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal" dir="ltr">If you want it back later (for your primary account's long-lived headless auth to keep working), you'll need to run <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">claude setup-token</code> again — it's genuinely removed, not just hidden.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div data-cds="MessageActions" data-deferred="" data-size="xs" class="flex items-center select-none ml-[calc(var(--msg-text-inset,0.5rem)_-_0.375rem)] -mt-1"><span aria-hidden="true" class="h-control w-px"></span><button type="button" class="sr-only" aria-label="Show message actions for Claude responded: Real, direct fix — the same [Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable command that set it, just with a null value, which removes it entirely."></button></div></div></div></div></div><div data-rs-index="1236" data-index="1236" data-testid="transcript-row" data-perf-row="human" data-perf-row-from-tail="1" data-perf-row-streaming="false" style="display: flow-root;
